generator, whether to sample independent thoughts (used in Creative Writing) or propose sequential thoughts (used in Game of 24)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--method_evaluate\u003c/code\u003e (choices=[\u003ccode\u003evalue\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003evote\u003c/code\u003e]): state evaluator, whether to use the value states independently (used in Game of 24) or vote on states together (used in Creative Writing)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--n_generate_sample\u003c/code\u003e: number of times to prompt for thought generation\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--n_evaluate_sample\u003c/code\u003e: number of times to prompt for state evaluation\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--n_select_sample\u003c/code\u003e: number of states to keep from each step (i.e. \u003ccode\u003eb\u003c/code\u003e in the paper's ToT + BFS algorithm)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003ePaper Trajectories\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-paper-trajectories\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Paper Trajectories\" href=\"#paper-trajectories\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003elogs/\u003c/code\u003e contains all the trajectories from the paper's experiments, except for \u003ccode\u003elogs/game24/gpt-4_0.7_propose1_value3_greedy5_start900_end1000.json\u003c/code\u003e which was reproduced after the paper (as the original experiment was done in a notebook) and achieved a 69% score instead of the original 74% score due to randomness in GPT decoding. We hope to aggregate multiple runs in the future to account for sampling randomness and update the paper, but this shouldn't affect the main conclusions of the paper.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eHow to Add A New Task\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-how-to-add-a-new-task\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: How to Add A New Task\" href=\"#how-to-add-a-new-task\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSetting up a new task is easy, and mainly involves two steps.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSet up a new task class in \u003ccode\u003etot/tasks/\u003c/code\u003e and task files in \u003ccode\u003etot/data/\u003c/code\u003e. See \u003ccode\u003etot/tasks/game24.py\u003c/code\u003e for an example. Add the task to \u003ccode\u003etot/tasks/__init__.py\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSet up task-specific prompts in \u003ccode\u003etot/prompts/\u003c/code\u003e. See \u003ccode\u003etot/prompts/game24.py\u003c/code\u003e for an example. Depending on the nature of the task, choose \u003ccode\u003e--method_generate\u003c/code\u003e (choices=[\u003ccode\u003esample\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003epropose\u003c/code\u003e]) and \u003ccode\u003e--method_evaluate\u003c/code\u003e (choices=[\u003ccode\u003evalue\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003evote\u003c/code\u003e]) and their corresponding prompts.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCitations\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-citations\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Citations\" href=\"#citations\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePlease cite the paper and star this repo if you use ToT and find it interesting/useful, thanks! <p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/princeton-nlp/tree-of-thought-llm/blob/master/pics/teaser.png"><img src="/princeton-nlp/tree-of-thought-llm/raw/master/pics/teaser.png" alt="teaser" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">Official implementation for paper <a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.10601" rel="nofollow">Tree of Thoughts: Deliberate Problem Solving with Large Language Models</a> with code, prompts, model outputs.
Also check <a href="https://twitter.com/ShunyuYao12/status/1659357547474681857" rel="nofollow">its tweet thread</a> in 1min.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Setup</h2><a id="user-content-setup" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Setup" href="#setup"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">Set up OpenAI API key and store in environment variable <code>OPENAI_API_KEY</code> (see <a href="https://help.openai.com/en/articles/5112595-best-practices-for-api-key-safety" rel="nofollow">here</a>).</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Install <code>tot</code> package in two ways:</p>
</li>
</ol>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Option 1: Install from PyPI</li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install tree-of-thoughts-llm"><pre>pip install tree-of-thoughts-llm</pre></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Option 2: Install from source</li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/princeton-nlp/tree-of-thought-llm
cd tree-of-thought-llm
pip install -r requirements.txt
pip install -e .  # install `tot` package"><pre>git clone https://github.com/princeton-nlp/tree-of-thought-llm
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> tree-of-thought-llm
pip install -r requirements.txt
pip install -e <span class="pl-c1">.</span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> install `tot` package</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The following minimal script will attempt to solve the game of 24 with <code>4 5 6 10</code> (might be a bit slow as it's using GPT-4):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import argparse
from tot.methods.bfs import solve
from tot.tasks.game24 import Game24Task

args = argparse.Namespace(backend='gpt-4', temperature=0.7, task='game24', naive_run=False, prompt_sample=None, method_generate='propose', method_evaluate='value', method_select='greedy', n_generate_sample=1, n_evaluate_sample=3, n_select_sample=5)

task = Game24Task()
ys, infos = solve(args, task, 900)
print(ys[0])"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">argparse</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">tot</span>.<span class="pl-s1">methods</span>.<span class="pl-s1">bfs</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">solve</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">tot</span>.<span class="pl-s1">tasks</span>.<span class="pl-s1">game24</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Game24Task</span>

<span class="pl-s1">args</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">argparse</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Namespace</span>(<span class="pl-s1">backend</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">'gpt-4'</span>, <span class="pl-s1">temperature</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0.7</span>, <span class="pl-s1">task</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">'game24'</span>, <span class="pl-s1">naive_run</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">False</span>, <span class="pl-s1">prompt_sample</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">None</span>, <span class="pl-s1">method_generate</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">'propose'</span>, <span class="pl-s1">method_evaluate</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">'value'</span>, <span class="pl-s1">method_select</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">'greedy'</span>, <span class="pl-s1">n_generate_sample</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>, <span class="pl-s1">n_evaluate_sample</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">3</span>, <span class="pl-s1">n_select_sample</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">5</span>)

<span class="pl-s1">task</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Game24Task</span>()
<span class="pl-s1">ys</span>, <span class="pl-s1">infos</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">solve</span>(<span class="pl-s1">args</span>, <span class="pl-s1">task</span>, <span class="pl-c1">900</span>)
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">ys</span>[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>])</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">And the output would be something like (note it's not deterministic, and sometimes the output can be wrong):</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="10 - 4 = 6 (left: 5 6 6)
5 * 6 = 30 (left: 6 30)
30 - 6 = 24 (left: 24)
Answer: (5 * (10 - 4)) - 6 = 24"><pre class="notranslate"><code>10 - 4 = 6 (left: 5 6 6)
5 * 6 = 30 (left: 6 30)
30 - 6 = 24 (left: 24)
Answer: (5 * (10 - 4)) - 6 = 24
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Paper Experiments</h2><a id="user-content-paper-experiments" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Paper Experiments" href="#paper-experiments"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Run experiments via <code>sh scripts/{game24, text, crosswords}/{standard_sampling, cot_sampling, bfs}.sh</code>, except in crosswords we use a DFS algorithm for ToT, which can be run via <code>scripts/crosswords/search_crosswords-dfs.ipynb</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">The very simple <code>run.py</code> implements the ToT + BFS algorithm, as well as the naive IO/CoT sampling. Some key arguments:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>--naive_run</code>: if True, run naive IO/CoT sampling instead of ToT + BFS.</li>
<li><code>--prompt_sample</code> (choices=[<code>standard</code>, <code>cot</code>]): sampling prompt</li>
<li><code>--method_generate</code> (choices=[<code>sample</code>, <code>propose</code>]): thought generator, whether to sample independent thoughts (used in Creative Writing) or propose sequential thoughts (used in Game of 24)</li>
<li><code>--method_evaluate</code> (choices=[<code>value</code>, <code>vote</code>]): state evaluator, whether to use the value states independently (used in Game of 24) or vote on states together (used in Creative Writing)</li>
<li><code>--n_generate_sample</code>: number of times to prompt for thought generation</li>
<li><code>--n_evaluate_sample</code>: number of times to prompt for state evaluation</li>
<li><code>--n_select_sample</code>: number of states to keep from each step (i.e. <code>b</code> in the paper's ToT + BFS algorithm)</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Paper Trajectories</h2><a id="user-content-paper-trajectories" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Paper Trajectories" href="#paper-trajectories"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>logs/</code> contains all the trajectories from the paper's experiments, except for <code>logs/game24/gpt-4_0.7_propose1_value3_greedy5_start900_end1000.json</code> which was reproduced after the paper (as the original experiment was done in a notebook) and achieved a 69% score instead of the original 74% score due to randomness in GPT decoding. We hope to aggregate multiple runs in the future to account for sampling randomness and update the paper, but this shouldn't affect the main conclusions of the paper.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How to Add A New Task</h2><a id="user-content-how-to-add-a-new-task" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How to Add A New Task" href="#how-to-add-a-new-task"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Setting up a new task is easy, and mainly involves two steps.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Set up a new task class in <code>tot/tasks/</code> and task files in <code>tot/data/</code>. See <code>tot/tasks/game24.py</code> for an example. Add the task to <code>tot/tasks/__init__.py</code>.</li>
<li>Set up task-specific prompts in <code>tot/prompts/</code>. See <code>tot/prompts/game24.py</code> for an example. Depending on the nature of the task, choose <code>--method_generate</code> (choices=[<code>sample</code>, <code>propose</code>]) and <code>--method_evaluate</code> (choices=[<code>value</code>, <code>vote</code>]) and their corresponding prompts.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Citations</h2><a id="user-content-citations" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Citations" href="#citations"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
